"Other programs keep their sound. Windows mixes every program in steps of {0} ms on this device, so the buffer cannot go below that. For lower latency choose the exclusive mode or an ASIO driver." = "其他程式保留其聲音。Windows在此裝置上以{0} ms為步長混合每個程式，因此緩衝區不能低於該值。要獲得更低延遲，請選擇獨佔模式或ASIO驅動程式。"
"This device does not support a shorter period in shared mode (it stays at {0} ms). Use the exclusive mode or an ASIO driver for lower latency." = "此裝置在共享模式下不支援更短的週期（保持在{0} ms）。要獲得更低延遲，請使用獨佔模式或ASIO驅動程式。"
"Shared like the normal mode, but asks Windows for a shorter mixing period. Only some drivers support it." = "與普通模式一樣共享，但向Windows請求更短的混合週期。僅部分驅動程式支援。"
"LYNX SHARE uses the device alone: the lowest latency without ASIO, but other programs go silent while LYNX SHARE plays." = "LYNX SHARE獨佔裝置：不用ASIO時延遲最低，但LYNX SHARE播放時其他程式會無聲。"
"Old Windows audio path with high latency. Use it only when the others fail." = "舊的Windows音訊路徑，延遲較高。僅在其他方式都失敗時使用。"
"Low-latency drivers. LYNX ASIO, FL Studio ASIO and ASIO4ALL work with any device; a manufacturer's ASIO driver works with its own device." = "低延遲驅動程式。LYNX ASIO、FL Studio ASIO和ASIO4ALL適用於任何裝置；廠商的ASIO驅動程式適用於其自己的裝置。"
"LYNX ASIO (built in)" = "LYNX ASIO（內建）"
"LYNX SHARE's own ASIO driver, with any device: playback and recording (microphone) at low latency. \"Driver settings...\" chooses shared or exclusive mode, the output and input devices and shows the latency. For other programs, run \"Register LYNX ASIO\" in LYNX SHARE's folder once." = "LYNX SHARE自己的ASIO驅動程式，適用於任何裝置：低延遲播放和錄音（麥克風）。「驅動程式設定...」可選擇共享或獨佔模式、輸出和輸入裝置，並顯示延遲。如需供其他程式使用，請在LYNX SHARE資料夾中執行一次「Register LYNX ASIO」。"
"LYNX SHARE's own ASIO driver, with any device: playback and recording (microphone) at low latency. \"Driver settings...\" chooses shared or exclusive mode, the output and input devices and shows the latency. To use it in other programs, install LYNX SHARE from lynx-share.com (the installer registers it)." = "LYNX SHARE自有的ASIO驅動程式，適用於任何裝置：低延遲播放與錄音（麥克風）。「驅動程式設定...」可選擇共用或獨佔模式、輸出與輸入裝置，並顯示延遲。若要在其他程式中使用，請從lynx-share.com安裝LYNX SHARE（安裝程式會註冊此驅動程式）。"

# --- Round 137: Echoform Rhythm mode and Era
"Rhythm echo: a pattern of evenly spaced taps that the feedback repeats every Length" = "節奏回聲：由均勻分佈的抽頭組成的樣式，回授每隔一個「長度」重複一次"
"Slots in the rhythm pattern (the first one is the input itself)" = "節奏樣式中的位置（第一個是輸入本身）"
"How much quieter the last tap of the pattern is than the first" = "樣式最後一個抽頭比第一個安靜多少"
"Length of the rhythm pattern in beats; the feedback replays it" = "節奏樣式的長度（拍）；回授會重播它"
"Repeats" = "重複"

"Audio thread: normal priority, so the sound breaks up more easily under load. To allow real-time priority, install rtkit (most desktops have it) or add yourself to the \"audio\" group with an rtprio limit (/etc/security/limits.d), then log in again. LYNX SHARE never needs root." = "音訊執行緒：普通優先順序，因此在高負載下聲音更容易斷續。要允許即時優先順序，請安裝rtkit（大多數桌面環境都有），或將自己加入帶有rtprio限制的「audio」群組（/etc/security/limits.d），然後重新登入。LYNX SHARE從不需要root權限。"

"The sound broke up, and Linux runs LYNX SHARE's audio thread at normal priority. LYNX SHARE never needs root. Options > Audio settings shows the current state." = "聲音出現斷續，而Linux以普通優先順序執行LYNX SHARE的音訊執行緒。即時優先順序可大大減少斷音：\n\n- 安裝rtkit（套件「rtkit」；大多數桌面環境已內建），或\n- 將自己加入「audio」群組，並在/etc/security/limits.d中設定rtprio限制（與JACK相同），\n\n然後登出並重新登入。LYNX SHARE從不需要root權限。「選項 > 音訊設定」會顯示目前狀態。"

# --- Round 163c: sample pitch (audio clips follow the pitch, keep length, Pitch...)
"The sample's pitch in semitones (0.5 = 50 cents). The Piano Roll's keys play it chromatically around its root note (Misc › Root note, C5 by default); right-click the channel › Pitch... for semitones + cents" = "取樣的音高，以半音為單位（0.5 = 50音分）。鋼琴捲軸的琴鍵圍繞其根音半音階地演奏它（雜項 › 根音，預設為C5）；在通道上按右鍵 › 音高... 可設定半音 + 音分"
"Keep length (stretch engine)" = "保持長度(拉伸)"
"On: the pitch, the fine tune and the keys change the pitch without changing the length, and \"Stretch\" changes the length without changing the pitch (like FL's Stretch modes). Off: resample, pitch and speed change together" = "開：音高、微調和琴鍵改變音高而不改變長度，「拉伸」改變長度而不改變音高（類似FL的Stretch模式）。關：重新取樣，音高和速度一起改變"
"The audio channel's pitch in semitones (0.5 = 50 cents): every clip of this file follows it. With \"Keep length\" the length stays, else the speed changes like a tape" = "音訊通道的音高，以半音為單位（0.5 = 50音分）：此檔案的每個片段都會跟隨它。開啟「保持長度」時長度不變，否則速度像磁帶一樣改變"
"On: the stretch and the pitch change separately (the stretch keeps the pitch, the pitch keeps the length). Off: resample, pitch and speed change together" = "開：拉伸和音高分別改變（拉伸保持音高，音高保持長度）。關：重新取樣，音高和速度一起改變"
"Pitch... (semitones, cents, keep length)" = "音高...（半音、音分、保持長度）"
"Transposes this audio clip's file (every clip of it follows)." = "對此音訊片段的檔案移調（其所有片段都會跟隨）。"
"Transposes the sample. The Piano Roll's keys still play it chromatically around its root note." = "對取樣移調。鋼琴捲軸的琴鍵仍會圍繞其根音半音階地演奏它。"
"Semitones (-24 to +24)" = "半音（-24到+24）"
"Cents (-100 to +100)" = "音分（-100到+100）"
"Off: resample, like a tape: higher is shorter, lower is longer." = "關：重新取樣，像磁帶一樣：越高越短，越低越長。"
"Clip menu: [LMB] or [RMB] opens it (pitch, fit to tempo, reverse, chop, make unique...)" = "片段選單：[LMB] 或 [RMB] 開啟（音高、適應速度、倒放、切碎、設為唯一...）"
"(pitch: [RMB] › Pitch...)" = "（音高：[RMB] › 音高...）"
